Redesign as if the requirement had been a foundational assumption from day one, instead of bolting it on.\\\"\\ndisable-model-invocation: true\\n---\\n\\n# Redesign From First Principles\\n\\nWhen integrating a change, don't bolt it onto the existing design. Redesign as if the requirement had been there from the start.\\n\\n- Read all affected files and understand the current design\\n- Ask: \\\"if we were writing this from scratch with this new requirement, what would we build?\\\"\\n- Propagate the change through every reference: types, docs, examples, rationale sections\\n- Think about the whole redesign, then deliver it incrementally\\n\\nThis is the method for preserving option value when integrating changes into an existing design.\\n\\n## Capability and permission boundaries\\n\\nUse the [session capability contract](../../references/host-capabilities.md) before reading source, editing files or running checks. Apply the redesign within the authorized task. Propagating references does not grant permission to change unrelated files or perform external actions.\\n\\nIf source is available but writes or execution are unavailable, provide a concrete redesign and incremental sequence as reduced capability. State which edits and checks were not performed. If affected source or design context is missing, identify the missing inputs and keep any redesign provisional. Claim implementation or verification only after observing the authorized operation.\\nexport function visible(orders,status){return orders.filter(o=>o.status===status)}\\nexport function exportCsv(orders){return \\\"id,customer,total\\\\n\\\"+orders.map(o=>`${o.id},${o.customer},${o.total}`).join(\\\"\\\\n\\\")}\\n[\\n  {\\n    \\\"id\\\": 1,\\n    \\\"status\\\": \\\"open\\\",\\n       "text": "{\n  \"caseId\": \"new-requirement\",\n  \"caseFile\": \"docs/parity/cases/skills/principle-redesign-from-first-principles.json\",\n  \"registry\": {\n    \"caseId\": \"new-requirement\",\n    \"caseFile\": \"docs/parity/cases/skills/principle-redesign-from-first-principles.json\",\n    \"sourcePaths\": [\n      \"skills/principle-redesign-from-first-principles/SKILL.md\"\n    ],\n    \"requiredHosts\": [\n      \"chatgpt\",\n      \"codex\"\n    ],\n    \"structuralOnly\": false,\n    \"hostStatus\": {\n      \"chatgpt\": \"not-run\",\n      \"codex\": \"not-run\"\n    },\n    \"requiredObservations\": {\n      \"pass\": [\n        \"read\",\n        \"review-outcome\"\n      ],\n      \"reduced-pass\": [\n        \"read\"\n      ]\n    },\n    \"executionModes\": {\n      \"pass\": [\n        \"direct\",\n        \"workers\"\n      ],\n      \"reduced-pass\": [\n        \"direct\",\n        \"sequential\",\n        \"workers\"\n      ]\n    },\n    \"requiredPermissions\": {\n      \"pass\": [],\n      \"reduced-pass\": []\n    }\n  },\n  \"contract\": {\n    \"id\": \"new-requirement\",\n    \"sourcePaths\": [\n      \"skills/principle-redesign-from-first-principles/SKILL.md\"\n    ],\n    \"prompt\": \"Add multi-tenant ownership to an existing single-tenant design.     {
      "path": "grades/web-baseline-52-103/projections/new-requirement/task-input/inputs/invoice.mjs",
      "bytes": 371,
      "sha256": "64adb438025e87e8e1a9d217d26ea510fdd11a8c7e27f25b74de70481396d1d8",
      "owner": "web-013-initial-new-requirement",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "export function calculate(rows){return rows.reduce((n,r)=>n+r.qty*r.unitPrice,0)}\nexport function adapter(invoice){return calculate(invoice.rows)}\nexport function facade(invoice){return adapter(invoice)}\nexport function controller(invoice){return {total:facade(invoice)}}\nexport function format(n){if(!Number.isFinite(n))throw new Error(\"nonfinite\");return n.toFixed(2)}\n"
    },

    {

      "owner": "web-013-initial-new-requirement",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "export function visible(orders,status){return orders.filter(o=>o.status===status)}\nexport function exportCsv(orders){return \"id,customer,total\\n\"+orders.map(o=>`${o.id},${o.customer},${o.total}`).join(\"\\n\")}\n"

Use for /arena, 'arena this', 'throw it in the arena', or when one attempt at a non-trivial artifact would lock in the wrong shape.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/arena/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/automate-me\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:automate-me\",\n      \"description\": \"Use for \\\"automate me\\\", \\\"create/update/refresh my -mode skill\\\", \\\"turn/capture my preferences or working style into a skill\\\", or wanting agents to follow how the user works. Drafts or revises a personal -mode skill via create-skill + unslop, optionally pulling fresh evidence from recent transcripts.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/automate-me/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/benchmark-checklist\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:benchmark-checklist\",\n      \"description\": \"Vet a perf measurement (limiter, tuning, limits, errors, repeatability, relevance, and whether the work happened) before you report or act on it. Use when you run a benchmark or report a speedup or regression you measured.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/benchmark-checklist/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/blast-radius\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:blast-radius\",\n      \"description\": \"Find what a change could break somewhere else before it ships, beyond the diff, and prove the one fact it's safe because of by running real code instead of writing it up. Use for 'blast radius of X', 'what could this break', or reviewing a small diff you don't trust.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/blast-radius/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/bro\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:bro\",\n      \"description\": \"Restate the last message in plain human language, with no jargon.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/bro/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/correct\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:correct\",\n      \"description\": \"Find the mistakes agents keep repeating in this repo and make each one impossible. Try architecture first, then types, then a lint whose error names the fix, then a test, and write docs last. Prove each check fails on a real past mistake. Repeat this each time the operator corrects you. Use for /correct.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/correct/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/create-verification-skill\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:create-verification-skill\",\n      \"description\": \"Generate a project-local verification skill that drives your app the way a user does, any language, framework, or platform. Use for /create-verification-skill, \\\"make a control skill for this repo\\\", or when a project has no scripted way to prove UI/CLI/service behavior.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/create-verification-skill/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/figure-it-out\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:figure-it-out\",\n      \"description\": \"Design an auditable playbook when no narrower one fits: a large migration, an ambitious multi-part change, or work a human reviews after stepping away. Scales rigor to the task, runs a hypothesis loop, and logs decisions via show-me-your-work. Use for /figure-it-out, 'figure it out', a large migration, or when no narrower playbook applies.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/figure-it-out/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/how\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:how\",\n      \"description\": \"Use for \\\"how does X work\\\", code walkthroughs before changing something, and placement / ownership / layering questions (\\\"where should this live\\\", \\\"which package owns this\\\", \\\"is this the right layer\\\"). Explains subsystem architecture, runtime flow, onboarding mental models. Use why for motivation.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/how/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/interrogate\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:interrogate\",\n      \"description\": \"Use for \\\"interrogate\\\", \\\"adversarial review\\\", \\\"multi-model review\\\", \\\"challenge this\\\", \\\"stress test this code\\\", \\\"find blind spots\\\", or \\\"tear this apart\\\". Multiple LLM reviewers challenge changes from independent angles.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/interrogate/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/maintain-verification-skill\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:maintain-verification-skill\",\n      \"description\": \"Periodic pass that keeps a project's verification skill and feature map honest: parallel source readers per feature, one live session driving every feature, at most one PR of proven corrections. Use for /maintain-verification-skill or \\\"audit the verify skill\\\".\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/maintain-verification-skill/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/no-comments\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:no-comments\",\n      \"description\": \"Spawn Comment Sicko, fix accepted findings, and offer encodings for claimed constraints.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/no-comments/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/poteto-help\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:poteto-help\",\n      \"description\": \"Guides users through pstack setup, /poteto-mode, and picking the skill, playbook, or principle for a task. Type /poteto-help with a question.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/poteto-help/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-attack-the-premise\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:principle-attack-the-premise\",\n      \"description\": \"Apply when two or more fixes that share one premise have failed the same gate. Take a census of which actors hold the imbalance before the next fix, then question the premise instead of writing another fix that assumes it.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-attack-the-premise/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-boundary-discipline\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:principle-boundary-discipline\",\n      \"description\": \"Apply when wiring validation, error handling, or framework adapters. Concentrate guards at system boundaries (CLI, config, network, external APIs); trust internal types and keep business logic in pure functions.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-boundary-discipline/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-build-the-lever\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:principle-build-the-lever\",\n      \"description\": \"Apply to any non-trivial work, not just bulk work: edits, migrations, analyses, checks. Build the tool that does it or proves it (codemod, script, generator, or a skill your subagents follow) instead of working by hand. The tool is the artifact a reviewer can rerun.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-build-the-lever/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-encode-lessons-in-structure\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:principle-encode-lessons-in-structure\",\n      \"description\": \"Apply when you catch yourself writing the same instruction a second time, or notice a recurring correction. Encode the rule as a lint, metadata flag, runtime check, or script instead of more text.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-encode-lessons-in-structure/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-exhaust-the-design-space\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:principle-exhaust-the-design-space\",\n      \"description\": \"Apply when facing a novel UI interaction or architectural decision with no precedent in the codebase. Build 2-3 competing prototypes and compare side by side before committing.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-exhaust-the-design-space/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-experience-first\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:principle-experience-first\",\n      \"description\": \"Apply when product, UX, or feature-scope tradeoffs come up. Choose user delight over implementation convenience; ship fewer polished features over more rough ones.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-experience-first/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-explain-the-number\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:principle-explain-the-number\",\n      \"description\": \"Apply before you trust, report, or act on a number you measured: a speedup, a regression, a throughput, a latency, or an eval result. Find what limits it, and rule out that it measured something other than the work you think.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-explain-the-number/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-fix-root-causes\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:principle-fix-root-causes\",\n      \"description\": \"Apply when debugging. Trace each symptom to its root cause and fix it there; reproduce first, ask why until you reach it, resist nil-check guards that silence crashes.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-fix-root-causes/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-foundational-thinking\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:principle-foundational-thinking\",\n      \"description\": \"Apply before writing logic: choosing core types and data structures, sequencing scaffold-vs-feature work, asking what concurrent actors share. Get the data structures right so downstream code becomes obvious.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-foundational-thinking/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-guard-the-context-window\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:principle-guard-the-context-window\",\n      \"description\": \"Apply when context is filling up: large outputs, long files, repeated reads, fan-out planning. Route bulk to subagents; keep summaries in the main thread, not raw payloads.\",\n      \"main_resource\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-guard-the-context-window/SKILL.md\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"authority\": {\n        \"kind\": \"cloud\"\n      },\n      \"package\": \"skill://plugins_6ad1c78a493c819197a589eb7a55361b/principle-laziness-protocol\",\n      \"name\": \"pstack-openai:principle-laziness-protocol\",\n      \"description\": \"Apply when refactoring, evaluating diff size, or tempted to add abstractions, layers, or signal threading.       "text": "{\n  \"caseId\": \"new-requirement\",\n  \"attemptId\": \"repair-new-requirement-0\",\n  \"owner\": \"/root/repair_tenant\",\n  \"status\": \"attempted\",\n  \"startedAt\": \"2026-10-16T19:15:41Z\",\n  \"completedAt\": \"2026-10-16T19:17:51.445Z\",\n  \"deadline\": \"2026-10-16T19:27:41Z\",\n  \"budgetEnforcement\": \"cooperative; completed before 8-minute reassessment point\",\n  \"source\": {\n    \"root\": \"/root/.codex/plugins/cache/created-by-me-remote/pstack-openai/0.1.3\",\n    \"version\": \"0.1.3\",\n    \"suppliedFreeze\": \"51f209daf83cd97d64f89881d66533bb59dc57db\",\n    \"binding\": \"installed file SHA256 evidence; supplied freeze not independently Git-proved\"\n  },\n  \"input\": {\n    \"root\": \"/mnt/data/pstack-013-repairs/subset/new-requirement/input\",\n    \"taskInputSha\": null,\n    \"gitIdentity\": \"no .git in supplied subset; no native commit created; controller source SHA is provenance only\"\n  },\n  \"actualResponse\": \"Replaced SKU-only ownership with validated tenant-bound handles over Map<TenantId, Map<Sku, quantity>>.       "text": "import {appendFileSync,writeFileSync} from 'node:fs';\nconst args=process.argv.slice(2);const started=new Date().toISOString();\nconst request={command:args,cwd:process.env.TASK_CWD??'/mnt/data/pstack-013-repairs/outputs/new-requirement'};\nappendFileSync('/mnt/data/pstack-013-repairs/outputs/new-requirement/raw.jsonl',JSON.stringify({phase:'request',at:started,request})+'\\n');\nconst p=Bun.spawnSync(args,{cwd:request.cwd,env:process.env});\nconst returned={exitCode:p.exitCode,stdout:p.stdout.toString(),stderr:p.stderr.toString(),signalCode:p.signalCode};\nappendFileSync('/mnt/data/pstack-013-repairs/outputs/new-requirement/raw.jsonl',JSON.stringify({phase:'returned',at:new Date().toISOString(),request,returned})+'\\n');\nprocess.stdout.write(returned.stdout);process.stderr.write(returned.stderr);process.exit(p.exitCode);\n"

    {
      "path": "grades/web-repairs/projections/new-requirement/working/domain.test.mjs",
      "bytes": 1398,
      "sha256": "9cef2dbf66992e53c449de584cce9d73c7ddedbaac2b1c666e4b2035ff28e845",
      "owner": "web-013-fresh-repair-new-requirement",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "import {test,expect} from 'bun:test';\nimport * as domain from './inventory.mjs';\nconst {TenantInventory}=domain;\ntest('ownership is nested, delimiter identities remain distinct and same tenant shares state',()=>{\n  const backend=new Map();const a=new TenantInventory('a:b',backend),b=new TenantInventory('a',backend),same=new TenantInventory('a:b',backend);\n  a.put('c',4);b.put('b:c',9);expect(same.get('c')).toBe(4);expect(a.get('b:c')).toBe(0);expect(b.get('b:c')).toBe(9);\n  expect(backend.get('a:b') instanceof Map).toBe(true);expect(backend.get('a:b').get('c')).toBe(4);\n  same.remove('c');expect(a.get('c')).toBe(0);expect(b.get('b:c')).toBe(9);\n});\ntest('public boundary requires and validates tenant, SKU, quantity and backend',()=>{\n  for (const id of [undefined,null,1,'','  ']) expect(()=>new TenantInventory(id)).toThrow();\n  const i=new TenantInventory('a');expect(i.tenantId).toBe('a');\n  for(const sku of ['',null,undefined,4,' ']) {expect(()=>i.put(sku,1)).toThrow();expect(()=>i.get(sku)).toThrow();expect(()=>i.remove(sku)).toThrow();}\n  for(const q of [-1,1.5,NaN,Infinity,'2']) expect(()=>i.put('s',q)).toThrow();\n  expect(()=>new TenantInventory('a',{})).toThrow();expect(()=>new TenantInventory('a',new Map([['a',3]]))).toThrow();\n  i.put('s',0);expect(i.get('s')).toBe(0);i.remove('missing');expect(i.get('missing')).toBe(0);\n  expect(domain.Inventory).toBeUndefined();\n});\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "grades/web-repairs/projections/new-requirement/working/example.mjs",
      "bytes": 339,
      "sha256": "17e151cd41144dd6bc2c07376ca20f8b0ec777cb30e60260a57bffb51c988109",
      "owner": "web-013-fresh-repair-new-requirement",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "import { TenantInventory } from './inventory.mjs';\nconst backend = new Map();\nconst first = new TenantInventory('company-a', backend);\nconst second = new TenantInventory('company-b', backend);\nfirst.put('sprocket', 7);\nsecond.put('sprocket', 2);\nconsole.log(JSON.stringify({first: first.get('sprocket'), second: second.get('sprocket')}));\n"

    {
      "path": "grades/web-repairs/projections/new-requirement/working/README.md",
      "bytes": 1573,
      "sha256": "a0a813fc6c94ca372ea90bd62dbf6e233e65de5f9178846e4938b31555f01b40",
      "owner": "web-013-fresh-repair-new-requirement",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "# Tenant inventory\n\nTenantInventory requires a nonempty string tenantId at construction. Supply a shared Map when instances must share storage. Ownership is structural: Map<tenantId, Map<sku, quantity>>. IDs and SKUs remain exact strings; colons and other delimiters have no special meaning. Each public CRUD operation validates its SKU; put also validates a nonnegative integer quantity. Missing items return zero and remove is idempotent.\n\n## Migration\n\nThe tenant-free Inventory export is deliberately removed. Replace new Inventory() with new TenantInventory(tenantId, sharedBackend), update imports and InventoryPort references to TenantInventoryPort, and migrate an old SKU map by placing it inside the appropriate tenant's inner map. Do not infer a default tenant. The original compatibility test is preserved outside working as stages/baseline-original.test.mjs; the migrated baseline retains CRUD and invalid-quantity coverage with explicit ownership. The supplied tenant acceptance file remains byte-identical.\n\n## Rationale and limits\n\nA tenant-first design makes ownership the first lookup, rather than encoding multiple identities into one string. Sharing a backend lets separately constructed tenant handles observe the same tenant data while keeping other tenants isolated. The backend is trusted process-local storage; direct caller mutation of the injected Map bypasses this API. No service, authentication or durable persistence is claimed. See example.mjs and types.ts for the public boundary. Types are declarations, not proof of compiler verification.\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "grades/web-repairs/projections/new-requirement/working/types.ts",
      "bytes": 550,
      "sha256": "4e3798d16d96c17aa96e7b433ad1dc4d31340e4c23b1075501698c40d8688a8f",
      "owner": "web-013-fresh-repair-new-requirement",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "export type TenantId = string;\nexport type Sku = string;\nexport type InventoryBackend = Map<TenantId, Map<Sku, number>>;\nexport interface Item { sku: Sku; quantity: number }\nexport interface TenantInventoryPort {\n  readonly tenantId: TenantId;\n  put(sku: Sku, quantity: number): void;\n  get(sku: Sku): number;\n  remove(sku: Sku): void;\n}\n// Constructor and mutators validate string identities and quantities at runtime.\nexport interface TenantInventoryConstructor {\n  new(tenantId: TenantId, sharedBackend?: InventoryBackend): TenantInventoryPort;\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "grades/web-repairs/projections/new-requirement/working/tenant.acceptance.test.mjs",
      "bytes": 649,
      "sha256": "15a5e3d1f1d1799b930cf9aea2ff44c7d906542188d8c35dca5cb22f704635a3",
      "owner": "web-013-fresh-repair-new-requirement",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "import {test,expect} from 'bun:test';import {TenantInventory} from './inventory.mjs';test('tenant-scoped ownership across shared backend',()=>{const shared=new Map();const a=new TenantInventory('tenant-a',shared),b=new TenantInventory('tenant-b',shared);a.put('same-sku',7);b.put('same-sku',2);expect(a.get('same-sku')).toBe(7);expect(b.get('same-sku')).toBe(2);a.remove('same-sku');expect(a.get('same-sku')).toBe(0);expect(b.get('same-sku')).toBe(2);a.put('tenant-b:sku',3);b.put('sku',8);expect(a.get('tenant-b:sku')).toBe(3);expect(b.get('sku')).toBe(8);expect(()=>new TenantInventory('',shared)).toThrow();expect(()=>a.put('s',-1)).toThrow()});\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "grades/web-repairs/projections/new-requirement/working/baseline.test.mjs",
      "bytes": 299,
      "sha256": "c05f414843b6df2780e189166a431b9540261f0b0280b574b1a8a243f2c681a1",
      "owner": "web-013-fresh-repair-new-requirement",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "import {test,expect} from 'bun:test';\nimport {TenantInventory} from './inventory.mjs';\ntest('migrated single tenant baseline',()=>{const i=new TenantInventory('explicit-company');i.put('s',3);expect(i.get('s')).toBe(3);i.remove('s');expect(i.get('s')).toBe(0);expect(()=>i.put('s',-1)).toThrow()});\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "grades/web-repairs/projections/new-requirement/working/inventory.mjs",
      "bytes": 1183,
      "sha256": "cb992b26e917a2a7a96412d1be11fb30da26cdadca2abc65c65bd6c001a3ed9f",
      "owner": "web-013-fresh-repair-new-requirement",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "function identity(value, label) {\n  if (typeof value !== 'string' || value.trim().length === 0) throw new TypeError(label + ' must be a nonempty string');\n  return value;\n}\n\nexport class TenantInventory {\n  #tenantId;\n  #backend;\n  constructor(tenantId, sharedBackend = new Map()) {\n    this.#tenantId = identity(tenantId, 'tenantId');\n    if (!(sharedBackend instanceof Map)) throw new TypeError('backend must be a Map');\n    const items = sharedBackend.get(this.#tenantId);\n    if (items !== undefined && !(items instanceof Map)) throw new TypeError('tenant storage must be a Map');\n    this.#backend = sharedBackend;\n  }\n  get tenantId() { return this.#tenantId; }\n  put(sku, quantity) {\n    identity(sku, 'sku');\n    if (!Number.isInteger(quantity) || quantity < 0) throw new TypeError('quantity must be a nonnegative integer');\n    let items = this.#backend.get(this.#tenantId);\n    if (!items) { items = new Map(); this.#backend.set(this.#tenantId, items); }\n    items.set(sku, quantity);\n  }\n  get(sku) { identity(sku, 'sku'); return this.#backend.get(this.#tenantId)?.get(sku) ?? 0; }\n  remove(sku) { identity(sku, 'sku'); this.#backend.get(this.#tenantId)?.delete(sku); }\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "grades/web-repairs/projections/new-requirement/increment-2.mjs",
      "bytes": 3988,
      "sha256": "f10f395e64ba35f58579f149e7e7e127fd0ff155a3fea53f2f7037554f299e3c",
      "owner": "web-013-fresh-repair-new-requirement",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "import {writeFileSync} from 'node:fs';\nconst w='/mnt/data/pstack-013-repairs/outputs/new-requirement/working/';\nwriteFileSync(w+'README.md',`# Tenant inventory\n\nTenantInventory requires a nonempty string tenantId at construction. Supply a shared Map when instances must share storage. Ownership is structural: Map<tenantId, Map<sku, quantity>>. IDs and SKUs remain exact strings; colons and other delimiters have no special meaning. Each public CRUD operation validates its SKU; put also validates a nonnegative integer quantity. Missing items return zero and remove is idempotent.\n\n## Migration\n\nThe tenant-free Inventory export is deliberately removed. Replace new Inventory() with new TenantInventory(tenantId, sharedBackend), update imports and InventoryPort references to TenantInventoryPort, and migrate an old SKU map by placing it inside the appropriate tenant's inner map. Do not infer a default tenant. The original compatibility test is preserved outside working as stages/baseline-original.test.mjs; the migrated baseline retains CRUD and invalid-quantity coverage with explicit ownership. The supplied tenant acceptance file remains byte-identical.\n\n## Rationale and limits\n\nA tenant-first design makes ownership the first lookup, rather than encoding multiple identities into one string. Sharing a backend lets separately constructed tenant handles observe the same tenant data while keeping other tenants isolated. The backend is trusted process-local storage; direct caller mutation of the injected Map bypasses this API. No service, authentication or durable persistence is claimed. See example.mjs and types.ts for the public boundary. Types are declarations, not proof of compiler verification.\n`);\nwriteFileSync(w+'example.mjs',`import { TenantInventory } from './inventory.mjs';\nconst backend = new Map();\nconst first = new TenantInventory('company-a', backend);\nconst second = new TenantInventory('company-b', backend);\nfirst.put('sprocket', 7);\nsecond.put('sprocket', 2);\nconsole.log(JSON.stringify({first: first.get('sprocket'), second: second.get('sprocket')}));\n`);\nwriteFileSync(w+'baseline.test.mjs',`import {test,expect} from 'bun:test';\nimport {TenantInventory} from './inventory.mjs';\ntest('migrated single tenant baseline',()=>{const i=new TenantInventory('explicit-company');i.put('s',3);expect(i.get('s')).toBe(3);i.remove('s');expect(i.get('s')).toBe(0);expect(()=>i.put('s',-1)).toThrow()});\n`);\nwriteFileSync(w+'domain.test.mjs',`import {test,expect} from 'bun:test';\nimport * as domain from './inventory.mjs';\nconst {TenantInventory}=domain;\ntest('ownership is nested, delimiter identities remain distinct and same tenant shares state',()=>{\n  const backend=new Map();const a=new TenantInventory('a:b',backend),b=new TenantInventory('a',backend),same=new TenantInventory('a:b',backend);\n  a.put('c',4);b.put('b:c',9);expect(same.get('c')).toBe(4);expect(a.get('b:c')).toBe(0);expect(b.get('b:c')).toBe(9);\n  expect(backend.get('a:b') instanceof Map).toBe(true);expect(backend.get('a:b').get('c')).toBe(4);\n  same.remove('c');expect(a.get('c')).toBe(0);expect(b.get('b:c')).toBe(9);\n});\ntest('public boundary requires and validates tenant, SKU, quantity and backend',()=>{\n  for (const id of [undefined,null,1,'','  ']) expect(()=>new TenantInventory(id)).toThrow();\n  const i=new TenantInventory('a');expect(i.tenantId).toBe('a');\n  for(const sku of ['',null,undefined,4,' ']) {expect(()=>i.put(sku,1)).toThrow();expect(()=>i.get(sku)).toThrow();expect(()=>i.remove(sku)).toThrow();}\n  for(const q of [-1,1.5,NaN,Infinity,'2']) expect(()=>i.put('s',q)).toThrow();\n  expect(()=>new TenantInventory('a',{})).toThrow();expect(()=>new TenantInventory('a',new Map([['a',3]]))).toThrow();\n  i.put('s',0);expect(i.get('s')).toBe(0);i.remove('missing');expect(i.get('missing')).toBe(0);\n  expect(domain.Inventory).toBeUndefined();\n});\n`);\nconsole.log('Increment 2 migrated docs, example, compatibility test; added domain tests. Acceptance file untouched.');\n"

      "path": "grades/web-repairs/projections/new-requirement/checklist.md",

      "sha256": "37f66d67a1c52b710aac074dc078b4f4ed338fa3685bd771305c98318be70506",

      "text": "# Checklist before task work\n- Read installed principle and host capability contract; initial wrong host-contract path failed, corrected read succeeded.\n- Read all six supplied affected files before implementation.\n- Copy exact inputs into owned working tree; hash immutable acceptance and original inputs.\n- Execute baseline compatibility green, then immutable tenant requirement red before redesign.\n- Compare current global-SKU design against structural tenant-first nested maps.\n- Deliver domain/types increment, then docs/example/rationale and tests, reading back each increment.\n- Run immutable acceptance plus full tests and example; preserve raw exits, compiler limitation and artifact hashes.\n- No commits, installs, services or source-package changes; cooperative deadline 19:27:41Z, reassess 19:23:41Z.\n"

    {
      "path": "grades/web-repairs/projections/new-requirement/increment-1.mjs",
      "bytes": 3050,
      "sha256": "3c04f074fe0d51283bc1d750fc5cd96c0d912e7454a9e960bbf65bfbabf8bb15",
      "owner": "web-013-fresh-repair-new-requirement",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "import {writeFileSync,copyFileSync,mkdirSync} from 'node:fs';\nconst root='/mnt/data/pstack-013-repairs/outputs/new-requirement';\nmkdirSync(root+'/stages',{recursive:true});\ncopyFileSync(root+'/working/baseline.test.mjs',root+'/stages/baseline-original.test.mjs');\nwriteFileSync(root+'/design.md',`Current identity is only SKU in a private per-instance map. Merely prefixing SKU with tenant would mix identity with serialization and create delimiter collisions. From scratch, ownership is Map<TenantId, Map<Sku, Quantity>>. TenantInventory binds a validated tenant ID at construction and addresses only its inner map. Shared storage is injected explicitly. No tenant-free public Inventory remains; migrating callers must provide tenant ID and share the backend when persistence sharing is intended. Pure synchronous CRUD remains the boundary; no services or global store. Increment 1 replaces domain and public types; increment 2 migrates callers, docs, rationale and tests. Backend ownership is trusted within the process; this is isolation by domain API, not protection against callers mutating the injected Map directly.\\n`);\nwriteFileSync(root+'/working/inventory.mjs',`function identity(value, label) {\n  if (typeof value !== 'string' || value.trim().length === 0) throw new TypeError(label + ' must be a nonempty string');\n  return value;\n}\n\nexport class TenantInventory {\n  #tenantId;\n  #backend;\n  constructor(tenantId, sharedBackend = new Map()) {\n    this.#tenantId = identity(tenantId, 'tenantId');\n    if (!(sharedBackend instanceof Map)) throw new TypeError('backend must be a Map');\n    const items = sharedBackend.get(this.#tenantId);\n    if (items !== undefined && !(items instanceof Map)) throw new TypeError('tenant storage must be a Map');\n    this.#backend = sharedBackend;\n  }\n  get tenantId() { return this.#tenantId; }\n  put(sku, quantity) {\n    identity(sku, 'sku');\n    if (!Number.isInteger(quantity) || quantity < 0) throw new TypeError('quantity must be a nonnegative integer');\n    let items = this.#backend.get(this.#tenantId);\n    if (!items) { items = new Map(); this.#backend.set(this.#tenantId, items); }\n    items.set(sku, quantity);\n  }\n  get(sku) { identity(sku, 'sku'); return this.#backend.get(this.#tenantId)?.get(sku) ?? 0; }\n  remove(sku) { identity(sku, 'sku'); this.#backend.get(this.#tenantId)?.delete(sku); }\n}\n`);\nwriteFileSync(root+'/working/types.ts',`export type TenantId = string;\nexport type Sku = string;\nexport type InventoryBackend = Map<TenantId, Map<Sku, number>>;\nexport interface Item { sku: Sku; quantity: number }\nexport interface TenantInventoryPort {\n  readonly tenantId: TenantId;\n  put(sku: Sku, quantity: number): void;\n  get(sku: Sku): number;\n  remove(sku: Sku): void;\n}\n// Constructor and mutators validate string identities and quantities at runtime.\nexport interface TenantInventoryConstructor {\n  new(tenantId: TenantId, sharedBackend?: InventoryBackend): TenantInventoryPort;\n}\n`);\nconsole.log('Increment 1 wrote domain, public types and comparison; saved original baseline.');\n"

      "path": "grades/web-repairs/projections/new-requirement/design.md",

      "sha256": "df3f0efb20656d3c0a2c1f0cc12e3fbd1d67fb926f8298438c2ad794f7159399",
      "owner": "web-013-fresh-repair-new-requirement",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "Current identity is only SKU in a private per-instance map. Merely prefixing SKU with tenant would mix identity with serialization and create delimiter collisions. From scratch, ownership is Map<TenantId, Map<Sku, Quantity>>. TenantInventory binds a validated tenant ID at construction and addresses only its inner map. Shared storage is injected explicitly. No tenant-free public Inventory remains; migrating callers must provide tenant ID and share the backend when persistence sharing is intended. Pure synchronous CRUD remains the boundary; no services or global store. Increment 1 replaces domain and public types; increment 2 migrates callers, docs, rationale and tests. Backend ownership is trusted within the process; this is isolation by domain API, not protection against callers mutating the injected Map directly.\n"
    },

0; }\\n  remove(sku) { identity(sku, 'sku'); this.#backend.get(this.#tenantId)?.delete(sku); }\\n}\\nexport type TenantId = string;\\nexport type Sku = string;\\nexport type InventoryBackend = Map<TenantId, Map<Sku, number>>;\\nexport interface Item { sku: Sku; quantity: number }\\nexport interface TenantInventoryPort {\\n  readonly tenantId: TenantId;\\n  put(sku: Sku, quantity: number): void;\\n  get(sku: Sku): number;\\n  remove(sku: Sku): void;\\n}\\n// Constructor and mutators validate string identities and quantities at runtime.\\nexport interface TenantInventoryConstructor {\\n  new(tenantId: TenantId, sharedBackend?: InventoryBackend): TenantInventoryPort;\\n}\\n\",\"stderr\":\"\"}}\n{\"phase\":\"request\",\"at\":\"2026-10-16T19:17:19.413Z\",\"request\":{\"command\":[\"/mnt/data/pstack-013-evaluation/runtime/bun-linux-x64/bun\",\"/mnt/data/pstack-013-repairs/outputs/new-requirement/increment-2.mjs\"],\"cwd\":\"/mnt/data/pstack-013-repairs/outputs/new-requirement\"}}\n{\"phase\":\"returned\",\"at\":\"2026-10-16T19:17:19.432Z\",\"request\":{\"command\":[\"/mnt/data/pstack-013-evaluation/runtime/bun-linux-x64/bun\",\"/mnt/data/pstack-013-repairs/outputs/new-requirement/increment-2.mjs\"],\"cwd\":\"/mnt/data/pstack-013-repairs/outputs/new-requirement\"},\"returned\":{\"exitCode\":0,\"stdout\":\"Increment 2 migrated docs, example, compatibility test; added domain tests. Supply a shared Map when instances must share storage. Ownership is structural: Map<tenantId, Map<sku, quantity>>. IDs and SKUs remain exact strings; colons and other delimiters have no special meaning. Each public CRUD operation validates its SKU; put also validates a nonnegative integer quantity. Missing items return zero and remove is idempotent.\\n\\n## Migration\\n\\nThe tenant-free Inventory export is deliberately removed. Replace new Inventory() with new TenantInventory(tenantId, sharedBackend), update imports and InventoryPort references to TenantInventoryPort, and migrate an old SKU map by placing it inside the appropriate tenant's inner map. Do not infer a default tenant. The original compatibility test is preserved outside working as stages/baseline-original.test.mjs; the migrated baseline retains CRUD and invalid-quantity coverage with explicit ownership. The supplied tenant acceptance file remains byte-identical.\\n\\n## Rationale and limits\\n\\nA tenant-first design makes ownership the first lookup, rather than encoding multiple identities into one string. Sharing a backend lets separately constructed tenant handles observe the same tenant data while keeping other tenants isolated. The backend is trusted process-local storage; direct caller mutation of the injected Map bypasses this API. No service, authentication or durable persistence is claimed. See example.mjs and types.ts for the public boundary.
